└─$ =cat > ~/Desktop/Y4S1/sws/memlabs/Lab1/render.py << 'EOF'
import sys, os
from PIL import Image, ImageOps

# usage: python3 render.py <file> <width> <tile_height> <outdir>
path, width, th, outdir = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3]), sys.argv[4]
os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
data = open(path, 'rb').read()
tile_bytes = width * th * 4          # 4 bytes per pixel (BGRA)
n = len(data) // tile_bytes
for i in range(n):
    chunk = data[i*tile_bytes:(i+1)*tile_bytes]
    img = Image.frombuffer('RGBA', (width, th), chunk, 'raw', 'BGRA', 0, 1)
    img = ImageOps.flip(img)          # Windows bitmaps are stored bottom-up
    img.convert('RGB').save(f'{outdir}/tile_{i:03d}.png')
print('wrote', n, 'tiles to', outdir)
EOFcat ><< 'EOF'importfrom# usage: python3 render.py <file> <width> <tile_height> <outdir>path,[1]([2])([3])[4]os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)data('rb')()tile_bytes**# 4 bytes per pixel (BGRA)n() //for(n)chunk[i*()*]img('RGBA'()'raw''BGRA')img()# Windows bitmaps are stored bottom-upimg.convert('RGB').save(f'{outdir}/tile_{i:03d}.png')print('wrote', n, 'tiles to', outdir)EOF>

cat > findwidth.py << 'EOF'
import sys, re
import numpy as np
raw = open(sys.argv[1], 'rb').read()
runs = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(rb'\xff{200000,}', raw)]
for s in runs:
    ws = max(0, s - 1_000_000) // 4 * 4
    pix = np.frombuffer(raw[ws:ws + 2_000_000], dtype=np.uint32)
    f = {}
    for w in range(300, 2400):
        rows = len(pix) // w
        a = pix[:rows * w].reshape(rows, w)
        f[w] = (a[1:] != a[:-1]).mean()
    best = []
    for w in range(303, 2390):
        nb = [f.get(w + d) for d in (-3, -2, -1, 1, 2, 3)]
        if None in nb:
            continue
        med = float(np.median(nb))
        if med > 0.02:
            best.append((round(f[w] / med, 2), w))
    best.sort()
    print(f"window offset {ws}: {best[:5]}")
EOF

cat > render2.py << 'EOF'
import sys, os
from PIL import Image, ImageOps
path, offset, height, outdir = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3]), sys.argv[4]
widths = [int(w) for w in sys.argv[5:]]
os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
data = open(path, 'rb').read()
for w in widths:
    n = w * height * 4
    chunk = data[offset:offset+n]
    if len(chunk) < n: continue
    img = Image.frombuffer('RGBA', (w, height), chunk, 'raw', 'BGRA', 0, 1)
    ImageOps.flip(img).convert('RGB').save(f'{outdir}/w{w}_off{offset}.png')
print('done')
EOFcd ~/Desktop/Y4S1/sws/memlabs/Lab1cat ><< 'EOF'importfrompath,[1]([2])([3])v[4]widths[(w)[]]os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)data('rb')()forn**chunk[]if len(chunk) <img('RGBA'()'raw''BGRA')ImageOps.flip(img).convert('RGB').save(f'{outdir}/w{w}_off{offset}.png')print('done')EOF>

└─$ =cat > scan.py << 'EOF'
import sys
import numpy as np
raw = open(sys.argv[1], 'rb').read()
WIN = 1_000_000
for ws in range(0, len(raw) - WIN, WIN):
    pix = np.frombuffer(raw[ws:ws+WIN], dtype=np.uint32)
    if len(np.unique(pix[::50])) < 8:      # skip blank/uniform memory
        continue
    f = {}
    for w in range(500, 2100):
        rows = len(pix) // w
        a = pix[:rows*w].reshape(rows, w)
        f[w] = (a[1:] != a[:-1]).mean()
    for w in range(503, 2097):
        nb = [f[w+d] for d in (-3,-2,-1,1,2,3)]
        med = float(np.median(nb))
        if med > 0.05 and f[w] / med < 0.5:
            print(f"offset {ws} width {w} score {f[w]/med:.2f}", flush=True)
EOF

cat > ~/Desktop/Y4S1/sws/memlabs/Lab2/kp.py << 'EOF'
import sys
from pykeepass import PyKeePass
db, pw = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
kp = PyKeePass(db, password=pw)
for g in kp.groups:
    print("GROUP:", g.path)
for e in kp.entries:
    print("ENTRY:", e.path, "|", e.username, "|", e.password, "|", e.notes)
EOF
